# The Darvas screen — 2020-03-01 → 2026-09-11

> **LONG-RUN BACKTEST.** One continuous price archive (2019-03-01 → 2026-09-11, 1885 symbols, in `ROLLING_2019-03-01_to_2026-09-13/`); every Friday screen sees only bars up to its own Friday; the earnings gate reads only fiscal years ended on or before the last 31 March at each screen date; the conference-call read is excluded. The net run pays Angel One charges on every order and settles capital-gains tax every 1 April. **The universe is POINT-IN-TIME with a ROLLING radar:** membership is recomputed EVERY MONTH as the top symbols by the TRAILING month's traded value from NSE's official bhavcopies, with hysteresis (leave only past rank 900) — casualties are IN while they traded, new listings enter the month they earn their place; membership gates fresh entries only (`_membership_long.csv`); split/bonus adjustments are heuristic, all listed in `_adjustments.csv`. No slippage, stop exits at the stop price, fractional shares. Stored fiscal statements exist for 37% of this universe — a stock without statements cannot be blocked by the earnings gate.

## The rules — the skill, kept simple

₹100 starts the book, and **no qualified signal is EVER starved**: entries are funded from the portfolio's cash first, with fresh capital topping up any shortfall (every top-up dated and logged — the yardstick is the money-weighted IRR). There is **NO ceiling on the number of positions**: ten is only the sizing denominator — each fresh entry is one tenth of the book at entry. **Stocks only:** ETFs and funds are excluded from the universe outright (86 instruments filtered from this archive). Every Friday after the close, the full three-gate screen (weekly volume ≥1.5× the 12-week average WITH a rising price; last month's volume ≥1.5× the year's norm; ≥3 boxes with the last 3 midpoints rising) runs over the whole universe; entries at the next trading day's open; falling earnings power refused; a box whose stop sits more than 25% below the price refused. Stops (box bottom − max(0.3×height, 5% of bottom)) are checked daily and ratcheted up weekly. **Two exits, never more:** the stop itself, and the DEAD-MONEY rule — a stock that seals no higher box for six months is sold at that Friday's close, freeing capital and attention. A sold symbol returns only by passing the full screen again. NO pyramiding — doubling was built, measured and retired: the add-on structurally bought the newest box top with the stop a whole box lower.

## The headline — IRR, since capital is added when signals call

| | Money put in | Final value | IRR (money-weighted, per year) |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| **This system, NET of charges and capital-gains tax** | ₹2,943.08 (₹100 + ₹2,843.08 added across 218 top-ups) | **₹4,032.92** | **+5.64%** |
| Before charges and taxes | ₹2,968.97 | ₹4,431.01 | +7.22% |
| Nifty 50 (same window, pre-cost, pre-tax) | ₹100.00 | ₹212.91 | +12.30% |

*₹0.00 of tax has accrued on the final part-year's realised gains (due next April) — settling it today would leave ₹4,032.92; unrealised gains in the end book carry a further deferred liability. 6.52 years, 341 weekly screens.*

## What the frictions took (net run)

- **Transaction charges: ₹76.56** (Angel One equity delivery: STT 0.10% both sides, exchange and SEBI levies, 18% GST, stamp duty 0.015% on buys, delivery brokerage ₹0 until 31 Oct 2024 then 0.1%).
- **Capital-gains tax paid: ₹246.90** — 20% short-term (≤365 days), 12.5% long-term, settled each 1 April with lawful set-off and loss carry-forward.

## What it took to earn it

- **Maximum drawdown: -12.3%** (peak 2021-07-30 → trough 2023-03-31, weekly closes).
- **1661 closed trades**: 669 winners (40%), average winner +28.5%, average loser -10.4%.
- Best closed trade NBCC +289.7%; worst MAHABANK -27.1%.
- Median holding period 57 days.
- Cash share of equity averaged 64% (median 71%); fully in cash 8 of 341 weeks — when nothing qualifies, the money waits.
